      );\n    }\n    state.gc.barrier_back(scripts);\n    Some(handlers)\n}\n\nconst DISPATCH_BINDINGS: [ScriptBinding; 3] = [\n    ScriptBinding::Precall,\n    ScriptBinding::Normal,\n ScriptBinding::Postcall,\n];\n\n/// Looks up one handler name for many frames in a dispatch pass, reusing the\n/// interned handler key and the per-binding registry tables. \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n/// Call the WoW error handler and log to stderr.\npub fn call_error_handler(lua: &mut rilua::Lua, error_msg: &str) {\n    call_error_handler_state(lua.state_mut(), error_msg);\n}\n\n/// State-only variant for RustFn call sites that only hold `&mut LuaState`.\npub fn call_error_handler_state(state: &mut LuaState, error_msg: &str) {\n    let collected_error = augment_error_with_traceback(state, error_msg);\n    let _ = sink_lua_error(\n        state,\n        &collected_error,\n        LuaErrorEmitPolicy::FirstOccurrenceOnly,\n    );\n    let Ok(handler) = ensure_error_handler(state) else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let Val::Function(_) = handler else {\n        return;\n    };\n    let msg_ref = state.gc.intern_string(collected_error.as_bytes());\n    let _ = protected_call_state_with_policy(\n        state,\n        handler,\n        &[Val::Str(msg_ref)],\n        LuaErrorEmitPolicy::FirstOccurrenceOnly,\n    );\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\nenum LuaErrorEmitPolicy {\n    /// Do not record or mirror the error.\n    Never,\n    /// Mirror to stderr + GUI console only for the first normalized occurrence.\n    FirstOccurrenceOnly,\n    /// Mirror every occurrence to stderr + GUI console.\n    Always,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\nstruct LuaErrorSinkResult {\n    is_first: bool,\n}\n\n/// Canonical Lua error sink.\n///\n/// This is the single entry point that records a Lua error into simulator state\n/// (raw message + addon attribution + normalized counts) and optionally mirrors\n/// it to stderr/GUI console using a single normalized-occurrence policy.\nfn sink_lua_error(\n    state: &LuaState,\n    error_msg: &str,\n    emit_policy: LuaErrorEmitPolicy,\n) -> LuaErrorSinkResult {\n    use super::env::WowLuaAppData;\n\n    let Some(app) = state.app_data::<WowLuaAppData>() else {\n        return LuaErrorSinkResult { is_first: false };\n    };\n    let Ok(mut sim) = app.sim_state.try_borrow_mut() else {\n        return LuaErrorSinkResult { is_first: false };\n    };\n\n    sim.lua_errors.push(error_msg.to_string());\n    let addon_name = sim\n        .executing_addon_index\n        .or(sim.loading_addon_index)\n        .and_then(|idx| sim.addons.get(idx as usize))\n        .map(|addon| addon.folder_name.clone());\n    sim.lua_error_records\n        .push(crate::lua_api::state::LuaErrorRecord {\n            message: error_msg.to_string(),\n            addon_name,\n        });\n\n    let normalized = crate::lua_errors::extract_error_message(error_msg);\n    let entry = sim.lua_error_counts.entry(normalized).or_insert(0);\n    let is_first = *entry == 0;\n    *entry += 1;\n\n    let should_emit = match emit_policy {\n        LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Never => false,\n        LuaErrorEmitPolicy::FirstOccurrenceOnly => is_first,\n        LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Always => true,\n    };\n    if should_emit {\n        eprintln!(\"Lua error: {error_msg}\");\n        sim.console_output.push(format!(\"Lua error: {error_msg}\"));\n    }\n\n    LuaErrorSinkResult { is_first }\n}\n\n/// Route `addframetext(...)` output through the canonical Lua error sink.\n///\n/// This mirrors every emitted message to stderr and GUI console while also\n/// recording raw messages, normalized counts, and addon-attributed records.\npub fn report_addframetext_error(state: &LuaState, message: &str) {\n    let _ = sink_lua_error(state, message, LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Always);\n}\n\nfn augment_error_with_traceback(state: &mut LuaState, error_msg: &str) -> String {\n    if error_msg.contains(\"stack traceback:\") {\n        return error_msg.to_string();\n    }\n\n    let Ok(func) = state.load(\"return debug and debug.traceback and debug.traceback() or ''\")\n    else {\n        return error_msg.to_string();\n    };\n    let Ok(results) = protected_call_state(state, Val::Function(func.gc_ref()), &[]) else {\n        return error_msg.to_string();\n    };\n    let Some(traceback) = results\n        .into_iter()\n        .next()\n        .and_then(|value| val_to_string(state, value))\n    else {\n        return error_msg.to_string();\n    };\n    let traceback = traceback.trim();\n    if traceback.is_empty() {\n        return error_msg.to_string();\n    }\n\n    format!(\"{error_msg}\\n{traceback}\")\n}\n\npub fn protected_call_state(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    func: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n) -> Result<Vec<Val>, Val> {\n    protected_call_state_with_policy(state, func, args, LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Never)\n}\n\nfn protected_call_state_with_policy(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    func: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n    emit_policy: LuaErrorEmitPolicy,\n) -> Result<Vec<Val>, Val> {\n    let saved_top = state.top;\n    let call_base = saved_top;\n    let saved_ci = state.ci;\n    let saved_n_ccalls = state.n_ccalls;\n    let saved_call_depth = state.call_depth;\n\n    prepare_protected_call_stack(state, call_base, func, args);\n\n    let call_result = state.call_function(call_base, LUA_MULTRET);\n    match call_result {\n        Ok(()) => {\n            let results = (call_base..state.top)\n                .map(|idx| state.stack_get(idx))\n                .collect();\n            state.top = saved_top;\n            Ok(results)\n        }\n        Err(err) => {\n            let error_val = recover_call_state(\n                state,\n                saved_ci,\n                saved_n_ccalls,\n                saved_call_depth,\n                call_base,\n                err,\n            );\n            state.top = saved_top;\n            record_protected_call_error(state, error_val, emit_policy);\n            Err(error_val)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn prepare_protected_call_stack(state: &mut LuaState, call_base: usize, func: Val, args: &[Val]) {\n    state.error_object = None;\n    state.ensure_stack(call_base + 1 + args.len());\n    state.stack_set(call_base, func);\n    for (index, arg) in args.iter().copied().enumerate() {\n        state.stack_set(call_base + 1 + index, arg);\n    }\n    state.top = call_base + 1 + args.len();\n}\n\nfn record_protected_call_error(state: &LuaState, error_val: Val, emit_policy: LuaErrorEmitPolicy) {\n    if emit_policy == LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Never {\n        return;\n    }\n    if let Some(error_msg) = val_to_string(state, error_val) {\n        let _ = sink_lua_error(state, &error_msg, emit_policy);\n    }\n}\n\nfn recover_call_state(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    saved_ci: usize,\n    saved_n_ccalls: u16,\n    saved_call_depth: u16,\n    call_base: usize,\n    err: rilua::LuaError,\n) -> Val {\n    state.ci = saved_ci;\n    state.base = state.call_stack[state.ci].base;\n    state.n_ccalls = saved_n_ccalls;\n    state.call_depth = saved_call_depth;\n    if state.ci < rilua::vm::state::MAXCALLS {\n        state.ci_overflow = false;\n    }\n    state.close_upvalues(call_base);\n    state.error_object.take().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n        let r = state.gc.intern_string(err.to_string().as_bytes());\n        Val::Str(r)\n    })\n}\n\n/// Call a function through Lua's protected `xpcall` path.\n///\n/// A cached bridge captures `debug.traceback` before the failing Lua stack\n/// unwinds.\npub fn protected_lua_pcall_state(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    func: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n) -> Result<Vec<Val>, String> {\n    let bridge =\n        ensure_protected_lua_pcall_wrapper_factory(state).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;\n    let mut protected_args = Vec::with_capacity(args.len() + 1);\n    protected_args.push(func);\n    protected_args.extend_from_slice(args);\n    let results = protected_call_state(state, Val::Function(bridge.gc_ref()), &protected_args)\n        .map_err(|error| {\n            val_to_string(state, error)\n                .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"script error ({})\", error.type_name()))\n        })?;\n    match results.first().copied() {\n        Some(Val::Bool(true)) => Ok(results.into_iter().skip(1).collect()),\n        Some(Val::Bool(false)) => {\n            let error = results\n                .get(1)\n                .copied()\n                .and_then(|value| val_to_string(state, value))\n                .unwrap_or_else(|| \"script error\".to_string());\n            Err(error)\n        }\n        _ => Ok(results),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Host frame identity, not Lua-editable taint, selects the instruction owner.\npub(crate) fn call_frame_handler_state(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    frame_id: u64,\n    func: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n    event: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<Vec<Val>, String> {\n    #[cfg(feature = \"retail-12-0-5\")]\n    return super::execution_budget::call_frame_handler(state, frame_id, func, args, event);\n    #[cfg(not(feature = \"retail-12-0-5\"))]\n    {\n        let _ = (frame_id, event);\n        protected_lua_pcall_state(state, func, args)\n    }\n}\n\npub fn call_void_function_state(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n    func: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n) -> Result<(), String> {\n    call_function_state(state, func, args)\n        .map(|_| ())\n        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())\n}\n\nfn ensure_protected_lua_pcall_wrapper_factory(\n    state: &mut LuaState,\n) -> rilua::LuaResult<rilua::Function> {\n    let existing = registry_value(state, PROTECTED_LUA_PCALL_WRAPPER_FACTORY_KEY);\n    if let Val::Function(func_ref) = existing {\n        return Ok(rilua::Function::from_gc_ref(func_ref));\n    }\n\n    let factory = state.load(\n        r##\"\n        local func = ...\n        local argc = select(\"#\", ...) - 1\n        local args = { select(2, ...) }\n        local handler = debug and debug.traceback or function(err) return tostring(err) end\n        return xpcall(function()\n            return func(unpack(args, 1, argc))\n        end, handler)\n    \"##,\n    )?;\n    set_registry_value(\n        state,\n        PROTECTED_LUA_PCALL_WRAPPER_FACTORY_KEY,\n        Val::Function(factory.gc_ref()),\n    );\n    Ok(factory)\n}\n\nfn ensure_error_handler(state: &mut LuaState) -> rilua::LuaResult<Val> {\n    let existing = registry_value(state, ERROR_HANDLER_KEY);\n    if existing != Val::Nil {\n        return Ok(existing);\n    }\n\n    let func = state.load(\"return function(_msg) end\")?;\n    let call_base = state.top;\n    state.ensure_stack(call_base + 2);\n    state.stack_set(call_base, Val::Function(func.gc_ref()));\n    state.top = call_base + 1;\n    state.call_function(call_base, 1)?;\n    let handler = state.stack_get(call_base);\n    state.top = call_base;\n    set_registry_value(state, ERROR_HANDLER_KEY, handler);\n    Ok(handler)\n}\n\npub(crate) fn registry_value(state: &mut LuaState, key: &'static str) -> Val {\n    let key_ref = state.gc.intern_string_static(key.as_bytes());\n    state\n        .gc\n        .tables\n        .get(state.registry)\n        .map(|table| table.get_str(key_ref, &state.gc.string_arena))\n        .unwrap_or(Val::Nil)\n}\n\npub(crate) fn set_registry_value(state: &mut LuaState, key: &'static str, value: Val) {\n    let key_ref = state.gc.intern_string_static(key.as_bytes());\n    let registry = state.registry;\n    if let Some(table) = state.gc.tables.get_mut(registry) {\n        let _ = table.raw_set(Val::Str(key_ref), value, &state.gc.string_arena);\n    }\n    state.gc.barrier_back(registry);\n}\n\n/// Collect a Lua error into SimState for later retrieval.\npub fn collect_lua_error(state: &LuaState, msg: &str) -> bool {\n    sink_lua_error(state, msg, LuaErrorEmitPolicy::Never).is_first\n}\n",

totalMinutes % 60\n  while minute < 0 do\n    minute = minute + 60\n    hour = hour - 1\n  end\n  while minute >= 60 do\n    minute = minute - 60\n    hour = hour + 1\n  end\n  while hour < 0 do\n .widgets\n            .get(widget_id)\n            .and_then(|frame| frame.owner_addon)\n    }\n\n    fn build_event_call_args(\n        &self,\n        lua: &mut rilua::Lua,\n        widget_id: call_args: &[Val],\n    ) {\n        let start = Instant::now();\n        self.state.borrow_mut().executing_addon_index = addon_idx;\n        let call_result = call_budgeted_widget_handler(\n         })\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"#{widget_id}\"));\n        Some((frame_name, state.start_time))\n    }\n\n    fn log_event_dispatch(&self, trace_label: &Option<EventTraceLabel>, !event_dispatch_trace_enabled(event) {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        let state = self.state.borrow();\n        let frame_name = state\n            .widgets\n            .get(widget_id)\n            .map(|frame| {\n                let name = frame\n                    .name\n                    .clone()\n                    .or_else(|| frame.parent_key.clone())\n                    .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"#{widget_id}\"));\n                let object_type = frame\n                    .object_type_name\n                    .clone()\n                    .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"{:?}\", frame.widget_type));\n                let owner = frame\n                    .owner_addon\n                    .and_then(|index| state.addons.get(index as usize))\n                    .map(|addon| addon.folder_name.clone())\n                    .unwrap_or_else(|| \"?\".to_string());\n                format!(\"{name} [{object_type}] owner={owner}\")\n            })\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!(\"#{widget_id}\"));\n        Some((frame_name, state.start_time))\n    }\n\n    fn log_event_dispatch(&self, trace_label: &Option<EventTraceLabel>, event: &str, phase: &str) {\n        let Some((frame_name, start_time)) = trace_label else {\n            return;\n        };\n        eprintln!(\n            \"{} [EventTrace] {event} -> {frame_name} {phase}\",\n            crate::logging::elapsed_prefix(*start_time)\n        );\n    }\n\n    fn dispatch_event_to_frame(&self, widget_id: u64, event: &str, args: &[Val]) -> Result<()> {\n        {\n            let state = self.state.borrow();\n            if should_skip_startup_actionbar_dispatch(&state, widget_id, event)\n                || should_skip_mists_raid_frame_group_roster(&state, widget_id, event)\n            {\n                return Ok(());\n            }\n        }\n        let addon_idx = self.handler_owner_addon(widget_id);\n        if should_skip_mists_addon_player_login(&self.state.borrow(), addon_idx, event) {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        let trace_label = self.event_trace_label(widget_id, event);\n        let mut lua = self.lua.borrow_mut();\n        if !event_matches_unit_filter(lua.state(), widget_id, event, args)? {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        // Intrinsic precall/postcall bindings receive events alongside the normal script.\n        let handlers = get_scripts_for_dispatch(lua.state_mut(), widget_id, \"OnEvent\");\n        if handlers.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        self.log_event_dispatch(&trace_label, event, \"begin\");\n        let call_args = self.build_event_call_args(&mut lua, widget_id, event, args)?;\n        for handler in handlers {\n            self.call_widget_handler(\n                &mut lua, widget_id, addon_idx, \"OnEvent\", handler, &call_args,\n            );\n        }\n        self.log_event_dispatch(&trace_label, event, \"end\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Fire a script handler for a specific widget with per-addon taint restoration.\n    pub fn fire_script_handler(\n        &self,\n        widget_id: u64,\n        handler_name: &str,\n        extra_args: Vec<Val>,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        let addon_idx = self.handler_owner_addon(widget_id);\n        let mut lua = self.lua.borrow_mut();\n        let handlers = {\n            let state = lua.state_mut();\n            crate::lua_api::script_helpers::get_scripts_for_dispatch(state, widget_id, handler_name)\n        };\n        if handlers.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(());\n        };\n\n        let call_args = self.build_script_call_args(&mut lua, widget_id, extra_args)?;\n        for handler in handlers {\n            self.call_widget_handler(\n                &mut lua,\n                widget_id,\n                addon_idx,\n                handler_name,\n                handler,\n                &call_args,\n            );\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a script handler is registered for a widget.\n    pub fn has_script_handler(&self, widget_id: u64, handler_name: &str) -> bool {\n        let mut lua = self.lua.borrow_mut();\n        get_script(lua.state_mut(), widget_id, handler_name).is_some()\n    }\n\n    /// Resolve a clicked frame to the nearest EditBox in its parent chain.\n    pub(crate) fn resolve_editbox_focus_target(&self, clicked_frame: Option<u64>) -> Option<u64> {\n        use crate::widget::WidgetType;\n\n        let state = self.state.borrow();\n        let mut current = clicked_frame;\n\n        while let Some(frame_id) = current {\n            let Some(frame) = state.widgets.get(frame_id) else {\n                break;\n            };\n            if frame.widget_type == WidgetType::EditBox {\n                return Some(frame_id);\n            }\n            current = frame.parent_id;\n        }\n\n        None\n    }\n\n    /// Simulate a left-click on a frame by ID.\n    pub fn send_click(&self, frame_id: u64) -> Result<()> {\n        let editbox_target = self.resolve_editbox_focus_target(Some(frame_id));\n        let old_focus = self.state.borrow().focused_frame_id;\n\n        if let Some(editbox_id) = editbox_target {\n            if old_focus != Some(editbox_id) {\n                self.state.borrow_mut().focused_frame_id = Some(editbox_id);\n                if let Some(old_id) = old_focus {\n                    self.fire_script_handler(old_id, \"OnEditFocusLost\", vec![])?;\n                }\n                self.fire_script_handler(editbox_id, \"OnEditFocusGained\", vec![])?;\n            }\n        } else if let Some(old_id) = old_focus {\n            self.state.borrow_mut().focused_frame_id = None;\n            self.fire_script_handler(old_id, \"OnEditFocusLost\", vec![])?;\n        }\n\n        let button_val = self.lua_string(\"LeftButton\");\n        self.fire_script_handler(frame_id, \"OnMouseDown\", vec![button_val])?;\n        self.fire_script_handler(frame_id, \"OnClick\", vec![button_val, Val::Bool(false)])?;\n        self.fire_script_handler(frame_id, \"OnMouseUp\", vec![button_val, Val::Bool(true)])?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatch a slash command (e.g., \"/wa options\").\n    /// Returns Ok(true) if a handler was found and called, Ok(false) if no handler matched.\n    pub fn dispatch_slash_command(&self, input: &str) -> Result<bool> {\n        let input = input.trim();\n        if !input.starts_with('/') {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n\n        let (cmd, msg) = match input.find(' ') {\n            Some(pos) => (&input[..pos], input[pos + 1..].trim()),\n            None => (input, \"\"),\n        };\n        let cmd_lower = cmd.to_lowercase();\n\n        let mut lua = self.lua.borrow_mut();\n        let slash_cmd_list = LuaApiMut::get_global_val(&mut *lua, \"SlashCmdList\");\n        let Val::Table(slash_table_ref) = slash_cmd_list else {\n            return Ok(false);\n        };\n        let state = lua.state_mut();\n        let globals = state.global;\n\n        for (name, handler) in matching_slash_handlers(state, globals, slash_table_ref, &cmd_lower)\n        {\n            if !matches!(handler, Val::Function(_)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            let msg_val = create_string(state, msg);\n            let _ = call_rilua_function(&mut lua, handler, &[msg_val])?;\n            let _ = name;\n            return Ok(true);\n        }\n\n        Ok(false)\n    }\n}\n\nfn call_budgeted_widget_handler(\n    state: &mut rilua::vm::state::LuaState,\n    widget_id: u64,\n    handler_name: &str,\n    handler: Val,\n    args: &[Val],\n) -> std::result::Result<Vec<Val>, String> {\n    let event = if handler_name == \"OnEvent\" {\n        args.get(1).and_then(|value| val_to_string(state, *value))\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n    super::script_helpers::call_frame_handler_state(\n        state,\n        widget_id,\n        handler,\n        args,\n        event.as_deref(),\n    )\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::WowLuaEnv;\n    use rilua::LuaApiMut;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unit_aura_token_survives_collection_during_payload_construction() {\n        let env = WowLuaEnv::new().unwrap();\n For frames with two-point anchoring (e.g., TopLeft + BottomRight to parent), the stored dimensions are 0 because actual size comes from anchor resolution at render time. The `Pos` field does show computed coordinates via `compute_frame_rect()`.\n\n### Apply Button\n\nClicking **Apply** writes editable fields back to the `WidgetRegistry` (`update.rs:617-636`) and invalidates the layout cache, causing an immediate re-render.\n\n### Hit Test Details\n\n**File:** `src/iced_app/view.rs:20-78`\n\nThe hittable list is lazily built and cached. It includes frames that are:\n- Visible (`frame.visible == true`)\n- Mouse-enabled (`frame.mouse_enabled == true`)\n- Ancestor-visible (all parents up the chain are visible)\n- Not in the exclusion list: `UIParent`, `Minimap`, `WorldFrame`, `DEFAULT_CHAT_FRAME`, `ChatFrame1`, `EventToastManagerFrame`, `EditModeManagerFrame`\n\nFrames are sorted by strata then level. The hit test iterates in reverse (highest first), returning the topmost frame under the cursor.\n\n---\n\n## Dump Tree\n\nTwo variants with different output formats and data sources.\n\n### Connected: `wow-cli dump-tree`\n\n**Files:** `src/bin/wow_cli/main.rs:44-52, 212-221`, `src/iced_app/tree_dump.rs:44-79`\n\nConnects to a running `wow-sim` via Unix socket IPC. Shows **computed layout positions** from the live renderer.\n\n```bash\nwow-cli dump-tree                      # All frames\nwow-cli dump-tree --filter Button      # Filter by name substring\nwow-cli dump-tree --visible-only       # Visible frames only\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n```\n+- UIParent (Frame) @ (0,0) 814x792\n      [anchor] TOPLEFT -> $parent:TOPLEFT offset(0,0) -> (0,0)\n      [anchor] BOTTOMRIGHT -> $parent:BOTTOMRIGHT offset(0,0) -> (814,792)\n   +- SettingsPanel (Frame) @ (-53,34) 920x724 [hidden]\n   |     [anchor] CENTER -> $parent:CENTER offset(0,0) -> (407,396)\n   |  +- __tpl_107 (Frame) @ (-46,52) 910x703 [stored=0x0]\n   |  |     [anchor] TOPLEFT -> $parent:TOPLEFT offset(7,-18) -> (-46,52)\n   |  |     [anchor] BOTTOMRIGHT -> $parent:BOTTOMRIGHT offset(-3,3) -> (864,755)\n```\n\n**Output details:**\n- Tree connectors: `+-` for each frame, `|` for ancestor continuation\n- Position and size are **computed** from `compute_frame_rect()` (anchor-resolved)\n- `[stored=WxH]` appears when stored `frame.width`/`frame.height` differ from computed size (`tree_dump.rs:212-218`)\n- `[hidden]` for invisible frames\n- `[anchor]` lines show each anchor's source point, target frame/point, offsets, and resolved absolute position\n- `[texture]` line for frames with a texture path set\n- Anonymous frames with text content show the text as display name: `\"Cancel\"` instead of `(anon)`\n\n**Name resolution** (`tree_dump.rs:194-209`):\n- Named frames show their global name\n- `__anon_*`, `__fs_*`, `__tex_*` prefixed frames are treated as anonymous\n- Anonymous frames with `frame.text` show the quoted text (truncated to 20 chars)\n- Truly anonymous frames show `(anon)`\n\n**Filter** matches frame names only (case-insensitive substring). If a frame doesn't match but has descendants that do, it still appears to preserve hierarchy.\n\n### Standalone: `wow-sim dump-tree`\n\n**Files:** `src/main.rs:48-58, 178-184`, `src/dump.rs`\n\nLoads the full UI (Blizzard + addons) without starting the GUI, then dumps.\n\n```bash\nwow-sim dump-tree                                     # Full load\nwow-sim --no-addons --no-saved-vars dump-tree         # Fast: skip addons\nwow-sim dump-tree --filter ScrollBar                  # Filter by name\nwow-sim dump-tree --visible-only                      # Visible only\nwow-sim dump-tree --delay 500                         # Wait 500ms after startup events\n```\n\n**Output format (different from connected):**\n```\n=== Anchor Diagnostic ===\nAnchored: 5234, Unanchored: 1892\nTop unanchored keys: [(\"NormalTexture\", 312), (\"PushedTexture\", 287), ...]\n\n=== Frame Tree ===\nSettingsPanel [Frame] (920x550) visible keys=[TitleContainer, ...]\n  .TitleContainer [Frame] (920x18) visible\n    .Title [FontString] (0x14) visible text=\"Options\" font=\"GameFontNormal\" size=0\n```\n\n**Key differences from connected mode:**\n- Prints anchor diagnostic summary first (anchored/unanchored counts, top unanchored parent keys)\n- Shows **stored** `frame.width`/`frame.height`, not computed layout\n- Uses parentKey names with `.` prefix for anonymous children (e.g., `.NormalTexture`)\n- Shows `children_keys` list, font info for FontStrings, and text content\n- No anchor detail lines per frame\n- No tree connector graphics (uses indentation only)\n\n---\n\n## Debug Visualization\n\n**File:** `src/iced_app/app.rs`, `src/iced_app/render_textures.rs`\n\nOverlay rendering for debugging layout. The live renderer emits shader quads into the overlay batch for:\n- red frame borders\n- green anchor-point markers\n- the existing hover/cursor overlays\n\n### Activation\n\n**CLI flags** (on `wow-sim`):\n```bash\nwow-sim --debug-elements    # Both borders + anchor points\nwow-sim --debug-borders     # Red borders around all elements\nwow-sim --debug-anchors     # Green dots at anchor points\n```\n\n**Environment variables** (override CLI):\n```bash\nWOW_SIM_DEBUG_ELEMENTS=1    # Both borders + anchor points\nWOW_SIM_DEBUG_BORDERS=1     # Red borders only\nWOW_SIM_DEBUG_ANCHORS=1     # Green anchor dots only\n```\n\n---\n\n## Architecture\n\nAll three tools read from the same Rust `WidgetRegistry` (`state.widgets`). No data comes from Lua \u2014 the Lua API methods (e.g., `SetPoint`, `SetSize`) write to the registry, and debug tools read from it.\n\n```\nLua API calls \u2500\u2500> WidgetRegistry <\u2500\u2500\u252c\u2500\u2500 Inspector Panel (live, editable)\n                                    \u251c\u2500\u2500 dump-tree connected (live, computed layout)\n                                    \u251c\u2500\u2500 dump-tree standalone (one-shot, stored sizes)\n                                    \u2514\u2500\u2500 Debug overlay (live, shader quads + runtime toggles)\n```\n\n### Layout Resolution\n\n**File:** `src/iced_app/layout.rs`\n\nThe connected dump-tree and inspector use `compute_frame_rect()` which resolves anchors dynamically:\n1. Look up the parent's rect (recursive)\n2. For each anchor, resolve the relative frame's position\n3. Compute edges from anchor points + offsets\n4. Derive width/height from opposite edges, or fall back to stored `frame.width`/`frame.height`\n\nThe standalone dump-tree uses stored `frame.width`/`frame.height` directly without anchor resolution.\n",
